1import { execFile } from "node:child_process";
2import { promisify } from "node:util";
3import { writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
4
5type Kind = "ctx" | "add" | "del" | "none" | "hunk";
6
7interface FileStat {
8 add: number;
9 del: number;
10 path: string;
11}
12
13interface Cell {
14 n: number;
15 t: string;
16 k: Kind;
17}
18
19interface Row {
20 l: Cell;
21 r: Cell;
22}
23
24interface FileDiff {
25 path: string;
26 rows: Row[];
27}
28
29const exec = promisify(execFile);
30
31const empty: Cell = { n: 0, t: "", k: "none" };
32
33const esc_map: Record<string, string> = {
34 "&": "&amp;",
35 "<": "&lt;",
36 ">": "&gt;",
37 '"': "&quot;",
38};
39
40const css = `
41 body { font: 16px system-ui, sans-serif; max-width: 1200px; margin: 2rem auto; padding: 0 1rem; color: #222; }
42 h1 { margin-bottom: .25rem; }
43 .meta { color: #666; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; }
44 code { background: #f0f0f0; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; }
45 pre { background: #f7f7f7; padding: 1rem; border-radius: 8px; white-space: pre-wrap; }
46 table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
47 td, th { padding: 6px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; text-align: left; }
48 .add { color: #1a7f37; } .del { color: #cf222e; }
49 .diff { table-layout: fixed; font: 12px ui-monospace, monospace; border: 1px solid #ddd; }
50 .diff col.n { width: 3rem; }
51 .diff td { padding: 1px 6px; border: 0; vertical-align: top; }
52 .code { white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-all; }
53 .num { color: #888; text-align: right; user-select: none; }
54 .c-del { background: #ffebe9; }
55 .c-add { background: #e6ffec; }
56 .c-none { background: #f6f6f6; }
57 .hunk { background: #ddf4ff; color: #555; }
58`;
59
60const esc = (s: string): string => s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => esc_map[c] ?? c);
61
62const git = async (args: string[]): Promise<string> => {
63 const { stdout } = await exec("git", args, { maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
64 const ans = stdout.trim();
65 return ans;
66};
67
68const parse_stats = (raw: string): FileStat[] => {
69 const ans: FileStat[] = [];
70 for (const line of raw.split("\n")) {
71 const [a, d, path] = line.split("\t");
72 if (a === undefined || d === undefined || path === undefined) continue;
73 ans.push({ add: Number(a) || 0, del: Number(d) || 0, path });
74 }
75 return ans;
76};
77
78const parse_diff = (raw: string): FileDiff[] => {
79 const ans: FileDiff[] = [];
80 let cur: FileDiff | undefined;
81 let in_hunk = false;
82 let dels: Cell[] = [];
83 let adds: Cell[] = [];
84 let ln = 0;
85 let rn = 0;
86
87 const flush = (): void => {
88 if (!cur) return;
89 const { rows } = cur;
90 const len = Math.max(dels.length, adds.length);
91 for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
92 rows.push({ l: dels[i] ?? empty, r: adds[i] ?? empty });
93 }
94 dels = [];
95 adds = [];
96 };
97
98 for (const line of raw.split("\n")) {
99 if (line.startsWith("diff --git")) {
100 flush();
101 in_hunk = false;
102 cur = { path: line.split(" b/")[1] ?? "", rows: [] };
103 ans.push(cur);
104 continue;
105 }
106 if (!cur) continue;
107 const m = /^@@ -(\d+)(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)/.exec(line);
108 if (m) {
109 flush();
110 in_hunk = true;
111 ln = Number(m[1]);
112 rn = Number(m[2]);
113 const { rows } = cur;
114 rows.push({ l: { n: 0, t: line, k: "hunk" }, r: empty });
115 continue;
116 }
117 if (!in_hunk) continue;
118 const t = line.slice(1);
119 if (line.startsWith("-")) {
120 dels.push({ n: ln++, t, k: "del" });
121 continue;
122 }
123 if (line.startsWith("+")) {
124 adds.push({ n: rn++, t, k: "add" });
125 continue;
126 }
127 if (line.startsWith(" ")) {
128 flush();
129 const { rows } = cur;
130 rows.push({ l: { n: ln++, t, k: "ctx" }, r: { n: rn++, t, k: "ctx" } });
131 }
132 }
133 flush();
134 return ans;
135};
136
137const render_cell = (c: Cell): string => {
138 const { n, t, k } = c;
139 const ans = `<td class="num c-${k}">${n || ""}</td><td class="code c-${k}">${esc(t)}</td>`;
140 return ans;
141};
142
143const render_diff = (d: FileDiff): string => {
144 const { path, rows } = d;
145 let body = "";
146 for (const { l, r } of rows) {
147 const { k, t } = l;
148 body +=
149 k === "hunk"
150 ? `<tr><td class="hunk" colspan="4">${esc(t)}</td></tr>`
151 : `<tr>${render_cell(l)}${render_cell(r)}</tr>`;
152 }
153 const ans = `<h3>${esc(path)}</h3>
154<table class="diff"><colgroup><col class="n"><col><col class="n"><col></colgroup>${body}</table>`;
155 return ans;
156};
157
158const build_html = (meta: string[], files: FileStat[], diffs: FileDiff[]): string => {
159 const [hash = "", name = "", mail = "", date = "", subj = "", body = ""] = meta;
160 let rows = "";
161 let add = 0;
162 let del = 0;
163 for (const f of files) {
164 add += f.add;
165 del += f.del;
166 rows += `<tr><td>${esc(f.path)}</td><td class="add">+${f.add}</td><td class="del">-${f.del}</td></tr>`;
167 }
168 let diff_html = "";
169 for (const d of diffs) {
170 if (d.rows.length > 0) diff_html += render_diff(d);
171 }
172 const ans = `<!doctype html>
173<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${esc(subj)}</title><style>${css}</style></head>
174<body>
175 <h1>${esc(subj)}</h1>
176 <div class="meta">
177 <code>${esc(hash.slice(0, 8))}</code> by ${esc(name)} &lt;${esc(mail)}&gt;
178 on ${esc(new Date(date).toLocaleString())}
179 </div>
180 ${body ? `<pre>${esc(body)}</pre>` : ""}
181 <h2>${files.length} files changed, <span class="add">+${add}</span> <span class="del">-${del}</span></h2>
182 <table><tr><th>File</th><th>Added</th><th>Removed</th></tr>${rows}</table>
183 <h2>Diff</h2>
184 ${diff_html}
185</body></html>`;
186 return ans;
187};
188
189export const run_report = async ()=> {
190 const [meta, stat, diff] = await Promise.all([
191 git(["log", "-1", "--format=%H%x00%an%x00%ae%x00%aI%x00%s%x00%b"]),
192 git(["show", "--numstat", "--format=", "HEAD"]),
193 git(["show", "-U2", "--format=", "HEAD"]),
194 ]);
195 const html = build_html(meta.split("\0"), parse_stats(stat), parse_diff(diff));
196 await writeFile("report.html", html);
197 console.log("Wrote report.html");
198 return html
199};

202 console.error(e);
203 process.exit(1);
204});
